# Exhaustive file inventory

This appendix is the machine-enumerated ledger required by the investigation brief. It includes every path returned by `git ls-files` at the current revision. Excluded rows retain their reason; in-scope rows retain explicit inspection status. A generated row is not evidence that the file was semantically understood.

Generated 2026-09-14 from revision `e66120ddf`. Inventory counts: other 10, configuration/resource 71, source 609, documentation 92, excluded 62, test/fixture 500.

Status meanings: `Fully inspected`, `Partially inspected`, `Not inspected`, or `Excluded with justification`.
